0.0;\n    for (const auto& val : data) {\n        T y = val - c;\n        T t = sum + y;\n        c = (t - sum) - y;\n        sum = t;\n    }\n    return sum;\n}\n\n// Print results for floating-point arrays\ntemplate <typename T>\nvoid print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = \"Result\") {\n    if (data.empty()) {\n        printf(\"=== RESULTS ===\\n\");\n        printf(\"Name: %s\\n\", name);\n        printf(\"Elements: 0\\n\");\n        printf(\"=== END RESULTS ===\\n\");\n        return;\n    }\n\n    const size_t N = data.size();\n    \n    // Compute statistics\n    T Generate a random point inside a triangle using barycentric coordinates\nVec3 randomPointInTriangle(const Triangle& t, RandomGenerator& rng) {\n    val_t u = rng.rand();\n    val_t v = rng.rand();\n    if (u + v > 1.0f) {\n        u = 1.0f - u;\n        v = 1.0f - v;\n    }\n    Vec3 ab = t.b - t.a;\n    Vec3 ac = t.c - t.a;\n    return t.a + ab * u + ac * v;\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Ray-Triangle Intersection (Möller-Trumbore algorithm)\n// ============================================================================\n\nval_t rayTriangleIntersect(const Vec3& orig, const Vec3& dir,\n                            const Vec3& v0, const Vec3& v1, const Vec3& v2) {\n    Vec3 e1 = v1 - v0;\n    Vec3 e2 = v2 - v0;\n    Vec3 pvec = dir.cross(e2);\n    val_t det = e1.dot(pvec);\n\n    if (std::abs(det) < EPSILON) return std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\n\n    val_t invDet = 1.0f / det;\n    Vec3 tvec = orig - v0;\n    val_t u = tvec.dot(pvec) * invDet;\n    if (u < 0.0f || u > 1.0f) return std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\n\n    Vec3 qvec = tvec.cross(e1);\n    val_t v = dir.dot(qvec) * invDet;\n    if (v < 0.0f || u + v > 1.0f) return std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\n\n    return e2.dot(qvec) * invDet;\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Visibility Testing (Octree-accelerated)\n// ============================================================================\n\n// Check if a ray between two triangles is blocked by any other triangle\n// Uses octree for O(log N) instead of O(N) search\nbool isRayBlocked(const Vec3& from, const Vec3& to,\n                  const Octree& octree,\n                  size_t srcTriIdx, size_t dstTriIdx) {\n    Vec3 dir = to - from;\n    val_t rayLen = dir.norm();\n    if (rayLen < EPSILON) return true;\n    Vec3 dirNorm = dir / rayLen;\n\n    return octree.applyToTris(from, to, [&](size_t idx, const Triangle& tri) {\n        if (idx == srcTriIdx || idx == dstTriIdx) return false;\n\n        val_t dist = rayTriangleIntersect(from, dirNorm, tri.a, tri.b, tri.c);\n        if (dist > EPSILON && dist < rayLen - EPSILON) {\n            return true;  // Ray is blocked\n        }\n        return false;\n    });\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Form Factor Computation (Kij)\n// ============================================================================\n\n// Compute the cosine of angle between vector and triangle normal\nval_t cosPhi(const Vec3& v, const Vec3& normal) {\n    val_t vNorm = v.norm();\n    if (vNorm <= EPSILON) return ZERO;\n    return std::max(ZERO, v.dot(normal) / vNorm);\n}\n\n// Compute form factor Kij between triangle i (receiver) and triangle j (emitter)\nval_t computeKij(size_t idxI, size_t idxJ,\n                  const std::vector<Triangle>& triangles,\n                  const Octree& octree,\n                  RandomGenerator& rng) {\n    const Triangle& triI = triangles[idxI];\n    const Triangle& triJ = triangles[idxJ];\n\n    // Cull triangles facing the same direction\n    if (triI.normal().dot(triJ.normal()) > 0.99f) return ZERO;\n\n    val_t kij = ZERO;\n\n    for (int r = 0; r < NUM_RAYS; ++r) {\n        Vec3 pI = randomPointInTriangle(triI, rng);\n        Vec3 pJ = randomPointInTriangle(triJ, rng);\n\n        if (isRayBlocked(pI, pJ, octree, idxI, idxJ)) continue;\n\n        Vec3 v = pJ - pI;\n        val_t distSqr = v.squaredNorm();\n        if (distSqr < EPSILON) continue;\n\n        val_t cosPhiI = cosPhi(v, triI.normal());\n        val_t cosPhiJ = cosPhi(-v, triJ.normal());\n\n        if (cosPhiI <= ZERO || cosPhiJ <= ZERO) continue;\n\n        kij += (cosPhiI * cosPhiJ) / (PI * distSqr);\n    }\n\n    return kij * INV_NUM_RAYS;\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Tau (time delay) Computation\n// ============================================================================\n\nint computeTau(const Triangle& triI, const Triangle& triJ) {\n    val_t dist = (triI.center() - triJ.center()).norm();\n    return static_cast<int>(std::ceil(dist * INV_WAVE_SPEED));\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Room Response Simulation State\n// ============================================================================\n\nstruct SimulationState {\n    size_t numTriangles;\n    size_t numTimesteps;\n\n    std::vector<Triangle> triangles;\n // Reflected radiosity (T x N matrix)\n    std::vector<val_t> distances;   // Computed distances from source\n\n    Octree octree;                  // Spatial acceleration structure\n\n    size_t sourceIndex;\n\n    size_t idx2d(size_t i, size_t j) const { return i * numTriangles + j; }\n    size_t idxTN(size_t t, size_t n) const { return t * numTriangles + n; }\n};\n\n// form factors (Kij)...\\n\");\n\n    const size_t N = state.numTriangles;\n    const size_t numPairs = N * N;\n    if (numPairs == 0) return;\n\n    // The reference implementation feeds one sequential RNG through all (i, j)\n    // pairs in row-major order. To reproduce that stream exactly in parallel we\n    // split the pair index space into contiguous chunks, count the draws each\n    // chunk consumes (deterministic: DRAWS_PER_PAIR per non-culled pair), and\n    // give every chunk a private RNG copy positioned at its stream offset.\n    const int numThreads = omp_get_max_threads();\n    const size_t targetChunks = static_cast<size_t>(numThreads) * 32;\n    size_t chunkSize = (numPairs + targetChunks - 1) / targetChunks;\n    chunkSize = std::max<size_t>(chunkSize, 16);\n    const size_t numChunks = (numPairs + chunkSize - 1) / chunkSize;\n\n    // Pass 1: draws consumed per chunk (cheap, parallel)\n    std::vector<unsigned long long> chunkDraws(numChunks, 0);\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(dynamic, 4)\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < numChunks; ++c) {\n        const size_t begin = c * chunkSize;\n        const size_t end = std::min(begin + chunkSize, numPairs);\n        unsigned long long draws = 0;\n        for (size_t p = begin; p < end; ++p) {\n            const size_t i = p / N;\n            const size_t j = p - i * N;\n            if (i == j) continue;\n            if (!cullSameDirection(state.triangles[i], state.triangles[j])) {\n                draws += DRAWS_PER_PAIR;\n            }\n        }\n        chunkDraws[c] = draws;\n    }\n\n    // Pass 2: producer/consumer. One thread walks the stream sequentially,\n    // publishing the RNG state at each chunk boundary (discard() only performs\n    // the block twist, ~1000x cheaper than the ray casting it stands in for),\n    // while all threads consume chunks in order as their checkpoints appear.\n    std::vector<RandomGenerator> checkpoints(numChunks, RandomGenerator(42));\n    std::unique_ptr<std::atomic<int>[]> ready(new std::atomic<int>[numChunks]);\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < numChunks; ++c) ready[c].store(0, std::memory_order_relaxed);\n\n    std::atomic<size_t> nextChunk{0};\n    std::atomic<size_t> pairsDone{0};\n    const size_t progressStride = 100 * N;\n\n    #pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        #pragma omp single nowait\n        {\n            RandomGenerator rng(42);\n            for (size_t c = 0; c < numChunks; ++c) {\n                checkpoints[c] = rng;\n                ready[c].store(1, std::memory_order_release);\n                rng.discard(chunkDraws[c]);\n            }\n        }\n\n        for (;;) {\n            const size_t c = nextChunk.fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_relaxed);\n            if (c >= numChunks) break;\n\n            while (ready[c].load(std::memory_order_acquire) == 0) {\n                #pragma omp taskyield\n            }\n\n            RandomGenerator rng = checkpoints[c];\n            const size_t begin = c * chunkSize;\n            const size_t end = std::min(begin + chunkSize, numPairs);\n            for (size_t p = begin; p < end; ++p) {\n                const size_t i = p / N;\n                const size_t j = p - i * N;\n                if (i == j) continue;\n                state.kij[p] = computeKij(i, j, state.triangles, state.octree, rng);\n            }\n\n            const size_t len = end - begin;\n            const size_t done = pairsDone.fetch_add(len, std::memory_order_relaxed) + len;\n            if (done == numPairs) {\n                printf(\"  Progress: %zu/%zu triangles\\n\", N, N);\n            } else if (done / progressStride > (done - len) / progressStride) {\n                printf(\"  Progress: %zu/%zu triangles\\n\", (done / progressStride) * 100, N);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n'''\ns=s.replace(old_ff,new_ff)\n\n# ---- time delays ----\nrep(\"\"\"    printf(\"Computing time delays (Tau)...\\\\n\");\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {\"\"\")\n\n# ---- simulation ----\nold_sim = s[s.index(\"void runSimulation(SimulationState& state) {\"):s.index(\"// ============================================================================\\n// Distance Computation\")]\nnew_sim = r'''void runSimulation(SimulationState& state) {\n    printf(\"Running wave propagation simulation...\\n\");\n\n    const size_t N = state.numTriangles;\n    const size_t T = state.numTimesteps;\n    const val_t* __restrict kijM = state.kij.data();\n    const int* __restrict tauM = state.tau.data();\n    const val_t* __restrict areas = state.areas.data();\n    const val_t* __restrict rho = state.rho.data();\n    const val_t* __restrict radE = state.radE.data();\n    val_t* __restrict radB = state.radB.data();\n\n    // Timesteps are strictly ordered (radB[t] depends on radB[t - tau], tau >= 1\n    // for distinct triangles), but all triangles within a timestep are\n    // independent. The per-triangle summation order over j is preserved.\n    #pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        for (size_t t = 0; t < T; ++t) {\n            #pragma omp for schedule(dynamic, 4)\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\n                val_t sumB = ZERO;\n                const val_t* __restrict kijRow = kijM + i * N;\n                const int* __restrict tauRow = tauM + i * N;\n\n                for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {\n                    if (i == j) continue;\n\n                    int tauij = tauRow[j];\n\n                    // Skip if wave hasn't yet propagated from j to i\n                    if (static_cast<int>(t) < tauij) continue;\n\n                    val_t kij = kijRow[j];\n                    if (kij <= ZERO) continue;\n\n                    // Get radiosity from source triangle at time when emission occurred\n                    size_t srcTime = t - static_cast<size_t>(tauij);\n                    val_t radJ = radB[srcTime * N + j];\n                    if (radJ <= ZERO) continue;\n\n                    // Accumulate contribution: form factor * area * source radiosity\n                    sumB += std::min(kij * areas[j], ONE) * radJ;\n                }\n\n                // Update radiosity: reflection + emission\n                radB[t * N + i] = rho[i] * sumB + radE[t * N + i];\n            }\n            // (implicit barrier of the worksharing loop orders timesteps)\n\n            #pragma omp master\n            if ((t + 1) % 10 == 0 || t + 1 == T) {\n                printf(\"  Timestep %zu/%zu\\n\", t + 1, T);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n'''\ns=s.replace(old_sim,new_sim)\n\n# ---- distances ----\nold_dist = s[s.index(\"void computeDistances(SimulationState& state) {\"):s.index(\"// ============================================================================\\n// Validation\")]\nnew_dist = r'''void computeDistances(SimulationState& state) {\n    printf(\"Computing distances via cross-correlation...\\n\");\n\n    const size_t N = state.numTriangles;\n    const size_t T = state.numTimesteps;\n\n    // Transpose radB (T x N) to per-triangle time series (N x T) so that the\n    // correlation loops stream through contiguous memory.\n    std::vector<val_t> series(N * T);\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\n        for (size_t parallel for schedule(dynamic, 8)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\n        const val_t* __restrict sB = series.data() + i * T;\n        val_t maxCorr = ZERO;\n        int bestT = 0;\n\n        // Discrete cross-correlation to find time delay\n        for (size_t t = 0; t < T; ++t) {\n            val_t sum = ZERO;\n\n            for (size_t tt = t; tt < T; ++tt) {\n                val_t pB = sB[tt];\n                val_t pS = src[tt - t];\n                sum += pS * pB;\n            }\n\n            if (sum > maxCorr) {\n                maxCorr = sum;\n                bestT = Spawning the whole machine for a\n// few microseconds of work per barrier only adds scheduling jitter.\nint teamSizeForWork(size_t workItems, size_t grain) {\n    const int maxThreads = omp_get_max_threads();\n    const size_t pieces = std::max<size_t>(1, workItems / grain);\n    return static_cast<int>(std::min<size_t>(static_cast<size_t>(maxThreads), pieces));\n}\n\n// When the default team would occupy every hardware thread of a large machine,\n// leave ~3% of them free. To reproduce that stream exactly in parallel we\n    // split the pair index space into contiguous chunks, count the draws each\n    // chunk consumes (deterministic: DRAWS_PER_PAIR per non-culled pair), and\n    // give every chunk a private RNG copy positioned at its stream offset.\n    const int numThreads = omp_get_max_threads();\n    const size_t targetChunks = static_cast<size_t>(numThreads) * 32;\n    size_t chunkSize = (numPairs + targetChunks - 1) / targetChunks;\n    chunkSize = std::max<size_t>(chunkSize, 16);\n    const size_t numChunks = (numPairs + chunkSize - 1) / chunkSize;\n\n    // Pass 1: draws consumed per chunk (cheap, parallel)\n    std::vector<unsigned long long> chunkDraws(numChunks, 0);\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < numChunks; ++c) {\n        const size_t begin = c * chunkSize;\n        const size_t end = std::min(begin + chunkSize, numPairs);\n        unsigned long long draws = 0;\n        for (size_t p = begin; p < end; ++p) {\n            const size_t i = p / N;\n            const size_t j = p - i * N;\n            if (i == j) continue;\n            if (!cullSameDirection(state.triangles[i], state.triangles[j])) {\n                draws += DRAWS_PER_PAIR;\n            }\n        }\n        chunkDraws[c] = draws;\n    }\n\n    // Pass 2: producer/consumer. k < ringSize; ++k) {\n        readyTag[k].store(0, std::memory_order_relaxed);\n        freeTag[k].store(0, std::memory_order_relaxed);\n    }\n\n    std::atomic<size_t> nextChunk{0};\n    std::atomic<size_t> pairsDone{0};\n    const size_t progressStride = 100 * N;\n\n    #pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        const bool producer = (omp_get_thread_num() == 0);\n        RandomGenerator streamRng(42);\n        size_t produced = 0;\n\n        // Publish checkpoints for as many further chunks as the ring allows.\n        // Returns false if blocked on a slot whose chunk is not yet copied out.\n        auto produce = [&]() -> bool {\n            while (produced < numChunks) {\n                const size_t slot = produced % ringSize;\n                if (produced >= ringSize &&\n                    freeTag[slot].load(std::memory_order_acquire) != produced - ringSize + 1) {\n                    return false;\n                }\n                ring[slot] = streamRng;\n                readyTag[slot].store(produced + 1, std::memory_order_release);\n                streamRng.discard(chunkDraws[produced]);\n                ++produced;\n            }\n            return true;\n        };\n\n        for (;;) {\n            if (producer) produce();\n\n            const size_t c = nextChunk.fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_relaxed);\n            if (c >= numChunks) break;\n            const size_t slot = c % ringSize;\n\n            while (readyTag[slot].load(std::memory_order_acquire) != c + 1) {\n                if (producer) produce(); p < end; ++p) {\n                const size_t i = p / N;\n                const size_t j = p - i * N;\n                if (i == j) continue;\n                state.kij[p] = computeKij(i, j, state.triangles, state.octree, rng);\n            }\n\n            const size_t len = end - begin;\n            const size_t done = pairsDone.fetch_add(len, std::memory_order_relaxed) + len;\n            if (done == numPairs) {\n                printf(\"  Progress: %zu/%zu triangles\\n\", N, N);\n            } else if (done / progressStride > (done - len) / progressStride) {\n                printf(\"  Progress: %zu/%zu triangles\\n\", (done / progressStride) * 100, N);\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Chunks already handed to other threads still need their checkpoints.\n        if (producer) {\n            while (!produce()) cpuRelax();\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n'''\ns=s.replace(old_ff,new_ff)\n\n# ---- simulation: static partition matching first touch ----\nrep(\"\"\"    const int nt = teamSizeForWork(N * N, 16384);\n    #pragma omp parallel num_threads(nt)\n    {\n        for (size_t t = 0; To reproduce that stream exactly in parallel,\n    // the pair index space is split into contiguous chunks whose draw counts\n    // are known up front (DRAWS_PER_PAIR per non-culled pair), and every chunk\n    // is processed with a private RNG copy positioned at its stream offset.\n    const size_t P = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads());\n\n    // Chunk layout: a cheap first wave (so all threads start almost at once),\n    // full-size chunks in the middle, and a guided taper at the end.\n    const size_t baseChunk = std::max<size_t>(32, (numPairs + 16 * P - 1) / (16 * P));\n    const size_t smallChunk = std::max<size_t>(16, baseChunk / 8);\n    std::vector<size_t> chunkBegin;\n    chunkBegin.reserve(24 * P + 2);\n    for (size_t pos = 0; pos < numPairs;) {\n        const size_t remaining = numPairs - pos;\n        size_t size = (chunkBegin.size() < 2 * P)\n                          ? smallChunk\n                          : std::clamp(remaining / (2 * P), smallChunk, baseChunk);\n        size = std::min(size, remaining);\n        chunkBegin.push_back(pos);\n        pos += size;\n    }\n    chunkBegin.push_back(numPairs);\n    const size_t numChunks = chunkBegin.size() - 1;\n\n    // Checkpoints are published per group of GROUP chunks; a consumer skips\n    // ahead over the preceding chunks of its group itself. This keeps the\n    // sequential producer's per-chunk cost (copying a 2.5 KB engine state)\n    // negligible compared to the block-twist discard it performs anyway.\n    constexpr size_t GROUP = 8;\n    const size_t numGroups = (numChunks + GROUP - 1) / GROUP;\n    std::vector<unsigned long long> chunkDraws(numChunks, 0);\n    std::vector<RandomGenerator> checkpoints(numGroups, RandomGenerator(42));\n    std::atomic<size_t> groupsReady{0};\n    std::atomic<size_t> nextChunk{0};\n    std::atomic<size_t> pairsDone{0};\n    const size_t progressStride = 100 * N;\n\n    #pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        // Pass 1: draws consumed per chunk (cheap)\n        #pragma omp for schedule(dynamic, 8)\n        for (size_t c = 0; c < numChunks; ++c) {\n            unsigned long long draws = 0;\n            for (size_t p = chunkBegin[c]; p < chunkBegin[c + 1]; ++p) {\n                const size_t i = p / N;\n                const size_t j = p - i * N;\n                if (i == j) continue;\n                if (!cullSameDirection(state.triangles[i], state.triangles[j])) {\n                    draws += DRAWS_PER_PAIR;\n                }\n            }\n            chunkDraws[c] = draws;\n        }\n\n        // Pass 2a (thread 0): walk the stream once, publishing group checkpoints.\n        // discard() only performs the block twist, ~700x cheaper per draw than\n        // the ray casting it stands in for, so the producer stays well ahead of\n        // the consumers and then joins them.\n        if (omp_get_thread_num() == 0) {\n            RandomGenerator streamRng(42);\n            for (size_t g = 0; g < numGroups; ++g) {\n                checkpoints[g] = streamRng;\n                groupsReady.store(g + 1, std::memory_order_release);\n                const size_t cEnd = std::min((g + 1) * GROUP, numChunks);\n                for (size_t c = g * GROUP; c < cEnd; ++c) {\n                    streamRng.discard(chunkDraws[c]);\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Pass 2b (all threads): consume chunks in order.\n        for (;;) {\n            const size_t c = nextChunk.fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_relaxed);\n            if (c >= numChunks) break;\n            const size_t g = c / GROUP;\n\n            while (groupsReady.load(std::memory_order_acquire) <= g) cpuRelax();\n\n            RandomGenerator rng = checkpoints[g];\n            unsigned long long skip = 0;\n            for (size_t cc = g * GROUP; cc < c; ++cc) skip += chunkDraws[cc];\n            rng.discard(skip);\n\n            const size_t begin = chunkBegin[c];\n            const size_t end = chunkBegin[c + 1];\n            for (size_t p = begin; p < end; ++p) {\n                const size_t i = p / N;\n                const size_t j = p - i * N;\n                if (i == j) continue;\n                state.kij[p] = computeKij(i, j, state.triangles, state.octree, rng);\n            }\n\n            const size_t len = end - begin;\n            const size_t done = pairsDone.fetch_add(len, std::memory_order_relaxed) + len;\n            if (done == numPairs) {\n                printf(\"  Progress: %zu/%zu triangles\\n\", N, N);\n            } else if (done / progressStride > (done - len) / progressStride) {\n                printf(\"  Progress: %zu/%zu triangles\\n\", (done / progressStride) * 100, N);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n'''\ns=s.replace(old_ff,new_ff)\nopen(p,'w').write(s)\nEOF\nmake -C /tmp/par_build 2>&1 | grep -E 'warning|error|Built'; {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-fable-5-1","id":"msg_011CgaZiuVUQrw9rb2gqcNCH","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"The roomsim benchmark is now parallelized with OpenMP, and results are bit-identical to the original at every problem size and thread count I tested. Only `roomsim/roomsim.cpp` and `roomsim/CMakeLists.txt` changed, the executable is still `roomsim`, and there are no new files or dependencies.\n\n**What changed**\n\n- **Form factors (over 99% of runtime).** The original feeds one sequential Mersenne Twister through all pairs, so naive parallelization would change the Monte Carlo results. Instead, the pair index space is split into chunks, the draw count per chunk is computed up front (each non-culled pair consumes exactly 64 engine outputs), and one thread walks the stream with the block-twist `discard`, publishing RNG checkpoints that all threads consume in order. Every chunk therefore uses exactly the random numbers it would have used sequentially. Chunks are small in the first wave and taper at the end for load balance.\n- **Time delays, simulation, cross-correlation.** Parallel over rows, with the per-row summation order preserved so floating-point results are unchanged. The two big N×N matrices are first-touched in parallel with the same static row partition the simulation uses, which fixed a memory-bandwidth bottleneck in the simulation phase. The cross-correlation reads a transposed copy for contiguous access.\n- **Thread-count handling.** Barrier-heavy phases size their team to the available work, and when the default team would fill every hardware thread the program leaves about 3% free. Both measures eliminated multi-hundred-millisecond barrier stalls I observed at 256 threads.\n- **CMake** links `OpenMP::OpenMP_CXX` via `find_package(OpenMP REQUIRED)`.\n\n**Verification.** Hashes from `-o` and the internal result hash match the original binary for n = 20, 80, 320, 1280 and 5120, at 1 to 256 threads, and for t = 200. Validation (`-v`) passes.\n\n**Timing on this 128-core / 256-thread EPYC box** (total computation time):\n\n| n | 1 thread | 64 threads | 128 threads | default (248) |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| 320 | 1460 ms | 29 ms | 23 ms | 33–46 ms |\n| 1280 | 26.6 s | 420 ms | 215 ms | 187 ms |\n| 5120 | 471 s | – | – | 3.07 s |\n\nScaling is essentially linear through 128 threads for n ≥ 1280. The remaining limit at n = 320 with all threads is a fixed region-start cost of a few milliseconds per OpenMP region, which dominates when the whole run is around 25 ms.\n\nOne assumption to be aware of: the parallel simulation relies on every off-diagonal time delay being at least one timestep, which always holds for distinct triangle centers. I added a cheap runtime check that falls back to the exact sequential sweep if that were ever violated."}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":32,"cache_creation_input_tokens":605,"cache_read_input_tokens":132311,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":605},"output_tokens":716,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"global","speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"a3f89998-c364-4dfe-b10f-20db1b80822f","uuid":"a30a0b03-8d75-410b-87b9-f40e788f6b38","timestamp":"2026-10-31T15:38:50.671Z","request_id":"req_011CgaZiuE6kJx1eD7cVwcqp"}
